# Tokengame

## EZ landing page (`ez/`)
- **THE LIVE SITE IS X3+ SINCE 2026-10-30 (owner, night: "push this to production with the last changes that I've made, so we have the comic version live... we're going to use this one for the main website for sure. The other one is just way too dry now"):** earningszn.xyz is built from `ez/v47-live-x3plus.html` (the light American comic); make_site.py builds X3+ by default now, and `SITE_PAGE=v46-live-x2plus.html` builds X2+ for its preview branch (`--branch preview`). X2+ stays as an artifact and preview. The X2+ production index from before the switch is in git (`site/` history) and was kept in the scratchpad as `x2plus-prod-index-backup.html`. Sheet syncs and films now go live as X3+ builds; data-only production deploys use X3+'s live index.html. On a 4x slower CPU here the cover lifts at about 31 s against X2+'s 22 s (X3+ still builds X2+'s whole page underneath); speed work is open.
- **Only two pages now (owner, 2026-10-21: "never update any other site anymore"):** EZ Live X2+ (`ez/v46-live-x2plus.html`, the preview artifact) and the main website (earningszn.xyz, built from X2+ by `ez/tools/site/make_site.py`). **Plus EZ Live X3+ since 2026-10-22 (owner: "a complete replica, but just with neon sign, call it X3 Plus"):** `ez/v47-live-x3plus.html`, https://claude.ai/artifact/H1uXHpDV6GmJcaef11n8RQ (private until the owner shares it), and https://x3plus.earningszn.pages.dev (a Cloudflare preview branch, never production). build.py writes it right after X2+: the same page with `tools/v22/skin-x3plus.css` in place of X2+'s origami lettering block (between `/*ORIGAMI{*/` and `/*}ORIGAMI*/` in skin-x2plus.css); so every X2+ change reaches X3+ too. **Letterpress since 2026-10-22, later (owner: "for X3 plus, can you do the letterpress"; it replaced the neon tubes):** the same broadcast straps and bugs, made of the "As it stands" ticket's lit card stock (build.py fills `__PAPER__` with that ticket's own texture), black edges, the words pressed in ink (Anton capitals), "before" and "after" in red ink (owner: the gold hid the F and FT on cream). The neon version is in git history. Not the older EZ Live X3 (v41). Republish it with X2+ on a sheet sync (same 231 files; publishes under 64 MB each); `SITE_OUT=<dir> SITE_PAGE=v47-live-x3plus.html python3 tools/site/make_site.py` then `wrangler pages deploy <dir> --branch x3plus`. **Ticket rip, a mockup on X3+ only (owner, 2026-10-22: "a mock-up for the scroll-driven" ticket, "on X3 plus, because we won't use that one in production"):** `tools/v22/x3plus-rip.js` and `x3plus-rip.css` (build.py adds them to X3+ only; X2+ stays byte for byte): an Admit One ticket (the hero line on it, lit card, scalloped sides, green and red bands, a perforation down the middle, "Scroll to tear it open") covers the first screen under the header; a spacer above the headline gives the tear about half a screen of ordinary scrolling (never hijacked): the tear spreads from the top, then the halves fly apart and fall while the headline and the explainer film rise into place, and the play button pulses; a tap on the ticket tears it for you; once torn, the spacer is taken out while the page is still (an instant scroll, the page itself scrolls smoothly) so the film stays exactly where it is. Not with reduced motion, another tab or an already scrolled page. Video of it: `ez/x3p-ticket-rip-mock.mp4` (rendered frame by frame). Do not republish or change EZ Season, X2, X3, X4, X5, X6, X7 or any other page, on syncs or otherwise; older lines below that say to republish them are history. build.py may still write their files.
- When the owner says **"sheet has been updated"** (or sends a new round film), follow `ez/SYNC.md` step by step. **Standing go (owner, 2026-10-23: "sheet updates and stuff, you can always push to production instantly... always flag something if something is out of order"):** sheet syncs, new round films and pictures go straight to earningszn.xyz (with both artifacts and both previews) once they check out; anything that does not match the sheet (a wrong number, a crown on a no-winner round, a winner the owner names that the sheet does not) is flagged to the owner, and a picture or film with a factual error is held, not published.

- **Leverage is 5x (owner, 2026-10-20: "instead of doing 10x on Hyperliquid, let's do 5x. I think that's better to get started with"):** SIM.leverage = 5 in ez-data.js; every page reads it, so a page shows 5x once its ez-data.js is republished (X2+ done 2026-10-20; the others still carry 10x until republished, and films with 10x baked in cannot follow). At 5x on Hyperliquid the trade is closed out before a 20% move against (about 17.5% on a market whose max leverage is 20x, like xyz:NVDA), so never write "about 20% wipes it out". The 10x lines below are history.
- **Owner decisions (2026-10-12):** the bet uses 10x leverage, stated as "10x", never "at least" (SIM.leverage = 10; this replaced the same morning's "at least 5x"; replaced by 5x on 2026-10-20); the leaderboard freezes 24 hours before NVIDIA reports and the AI in first place makes the call (SIM.leadHours; say it in those plain words, not "#1 locks"); no stop loss (SIM.stopLoss = null; a wrong call can wipe out the pot; at 10x on Hyperliquid the trade is closed out before a 10% move against, as early as about 5% depending on the market's max leverage, so never write "about 10% wipes it out"); - **Strategy (owner, 2026-10-19):** what EZ sets out to prove is that AIs can get better over time at calling a stock's move after earnings. The public, checkable record is the product; the pool gives it skin in the game. NVIDIA is the **first** bet (Season 1), not the end of the project. Season 2: bets during earnings seasons on companies that trade on Hyperliquid with enough open interest and volume, ideally about one a week (some weeks two, some none; never promise a fixed cadence). Hyperliquid only, so every bet is open and checkable. Each AI writes its lessons into a notebook file in its own instance after every round and reads it before the next call. The word is **"pool"** (not "pot") in new copy and the pin-tweet film. "Profits" = the trade's profit (e.g. a $10K gain buys back $10K of $EZ). Pending from the owner: a rule for moving part of the pool straight into buybacks when it grows too big for the markets to absorb (the owner mentioned 60% / half; numbers not settled), and how the leader is picked for Season 2 bets. Checked on 2026-10-19 via Hyperliquid's API: xyz:NVDA allows up to 20x (so 10x is possible), about $113M open interest; 48 of the 116 Season 1 companies have a Hyperliquid market, 18 with at least $10M open interest.
- **The holistic story (owner, 2026-10-19, overrides the detail above where they differ):** the grand question is "can an AI get good at predicting earnings?"; EZ wants to be the place people go to see what the AIs are predicting and their entire track record; a good record gets turned into buybacks. Bets grow season by season: Season 1 = one bet (NVIDIA); next season = more than one, at least every Mag 7 report; then more. Keep the technicalities (cadence, sizing, liquidity) off the pages for now. Every page must make clear NVIDIA is the first bet, not the only one; no page may read as "one NVIDIA bet each season".
- **First screen, concept first (owner, 2026-10-19, for X2's cel concept and the next X2 build):** keep the hero "We're putting buybacks in the hands of an AI." (the audience is crypto; buybacks stay up front; if EZ ever moves beyond crypto, some elements can change). The four bullets explain what EZ does, not the bet: "4 AIs predict stock moves after earnings." / "Every call is posted before the report." / "Graded at the next close. Lessons learned." / "69% of trading fees bet on the top AI's call." (one line each from 320 px). NVIDIA appears only under the chip: "Countdown to bet #1 · NVIDIA", then "Right: profits buy back $EZ. Wrong: the pool can be wiped out." The AIs are graded by the market close, not by themselves; they write their own lessons.

Live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-21 (owner go). **The EZ grade, rubric v1 (owner, 2026-10-21; live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-21, owner go):** one number from 1 to 10 replaces "4/4". Direction sets the band and the word (all right 8 to 10 "Perfect", most 6 to 7 "Strong", half 4 to 5 "Split", some 2 to 3 "Rough", none 1 "Rekt"); the round takes the top of its band when the AIs that called the direction missed by 2% or less on average; a 10 = all right, an average miss of 1.5% or less and under half the move (keeps tiny moves like Foxconn and BlackRock out), word "Gem", or "Pristine" under 1%. Gold label and foil go on 10s only, so they stay rare (3 so far: TSMC and RPM Pristine, GE Gem). The back of each card says why in one line where the front has the barcode ("4 of 4 right · 0.91% off on avg"). Thresholds are tuned on 32 rounds; adapt them as rounds come in, but say so (a published rubric that moves). **Half points (owner, later the same day: "I don't mind adding decimals"):** closeness of the AIs that called the direction adds 0 to 2 in steps of .5 (average miss 1.5% or less: +2, 2%: +1.5, 2.5%: +1, 3%: +.5); all right = 8 to 10 (a would-be 10 that fails the half-the-move rule is 9.5), most 6 to 7.5, half 4 to 5.5, some 2 to 3.5, none 1. PSA itself has halves only from 1.5 to 8.5 and no 9.5 (Beckett and CGC have 9.5); ours is our own scale. 32 rounds: 10 x3, 9.5 x3, 8 x2, 7.5 x2, 7, 6.5 x3, 6 x2, 5.5 x2, 5 x2, 4, 3.5 x5, 2.5 x2, 1 x4. - **Launch plan (owner, 2026-10-22):** launch on pump.fun (Solana) on a weekday, Monday 26 October (not the weekend; the week of 26 October has 38 calls incl. GOOGL, MSFT, HOOD, AAPL, AMZN, COIN, MSTR). Two tabs at launch, **Overview** and **Upcoming**; the Overview in this order: first screen (hero, the explainer film ticket, the bet ticket) -> the record (every call) -> current standings -> the stats switch (Hit rate / Avg miss / ROI / By sector, from the Leaderboard) -> the highlights (the most lopsided calls, from Results; they will carry our own PSA-style graded slabs) -> Buyback: only the NVIDIA card for now and a "more soon" line. No Hall of Fame (owner, later the same day). Whether the standings or the record comes first after the first screen is open (owner: "what must we research or prove"); the site has no event analytics yet, so only a test on real traffic can settle it. A clean first launch first: be very good at the cards; the notebook-versus-no-notebook control run is for later. The owner's working assumption: people care about an AI's calls only once it has a good record. Off at launch: the Leaderboard, Results and Buyback tabs, Unusual findings, and **model pages: no clicking into a model** (done 2026-10-22 on X2+: x2plus.js turns every `#/model/` link into plain text `.x2p-mn` and sends a model address to the Overview; the pages stay in the build). Then ship features one at a time, each a post on X ("we keep shipping" is the signal, not retention): Unusual findings, the buyback details (the pool growing, 5x on Hyperliquid), Results, a Leaderboard, and a **Models** tab: per model its lead module, then its notebook: the reasoning at call time (before the report), what changed since its last call, and the lesson it wrote after grading (capture these from the next calls on; they cannot be added after the fact). Content rule: only original data nobody else has; the more each model is asked to record, the more the page can show. The thesis: given freedom to research, grade themselves and keep a notebook they read before every call, do the AIs get better at calling earnings moves? The pool: after launch, fees split 69/31 into two wallets and the page reads the 69% wallet live (the owner's assumption, being verified: how pump.fun creator fees are paid and split, and how a static site reads a Solana balance). Earnings keep coming after 17 November (smaller reporters in December), so the sheet's empty stretch is not a real content desert.
- **Two-tab launch build (owner, 2026-10-22; live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-22, owner go, with everything below):** tabs Overview and Upcoming, plus a third that says what comes next (owner, same day: "Buybacks (soon)", option B of `ez/x2p-launch-tabs-*.png`): "Buybacks" in the tabs' own font (an `<a>` with no address, so it takes their styles and goes nowhere; dimmed text, `aria-disabled`), a small gold "Soon" tag; on phones the three share the row equally, on desktop they sit beside the logo as before; the selected tab is the tilted gold one. Light it up when the buyback ships (an X post). Otherwise only (x2plus.js removes the other nav links; #/leaderboard, #/models, #/results and #/buyback go to the Overview and send no page view). The Overview: first screen (A1) -> **01 Current standings**, the Leaderboard tab's board built on the Overview (build.py hands x2plus.js the page's renderers as `window.__x2pR` = vModels, wireModels, vResults; x2plus.js renders them out of sight and moves the parts in): no Avg miss column; the last five in their own column, halfway between the longest name and the leftmost hit rate, labelled "Last 5" once in the header like Hit rate and ROI (owner, 2026-10-22: L2; centred over the boxes by x2plus.js centre(), the header's screen-reader "Last 5" kept); the model name under the name, a touch smaller; a bare ⓘ (no box, words for screen readers only) turns the board to "How we rank" (`lbFlip`, shared with the old tab); the "As it stands" ticket under it. Rank, logo, name, last five, hit rate and ROI sit on each strip's middle by their ink (canvas metrics plus a baseline probe, +.5px; checked on the pixels with `ez/tools/concepts/launch-inkcheck.py`: within 0.4px on a phone and on desktop), recomputed on fonts, resize and tab change (owner: "a freak when it comes to symmetry"). -> **02 the record** -> **03 the proof** ("Getting smarter or just lucky?"): the Leaderboard's four stats in the owner's order Hit rate, ROI, Avg miss, Sector, opening on Hit rate; on narrow screens the hit-rate chart labels R1 and every fifth round (they ran into each other) -> **04 Highlights** ("The rounds CT would screenshot", the Results slabs), landing on Tightest race (TSMC, Pristine 10) with its story side up (`__x2pHlGo`) -> **05 Get $EZ** (kept by default for launch day; the owner did not mention it). Defaults taken while the owner decides: stats open on Hit rate (the owner first said ROI), Get $EZ stays, ROI stays on the board. "Every graded round" comes later. **Round 6 fixes (owner, 2026-10-22):** X2's Overview order routine (routes.js `apply()`, re-run on every tab change) put the sections back in X2's order and the highlights under Get $EZ; build.py now gives X2+ the launch order there (standings, record, proof, highlights, Get $EZ), X2 untouched. the views as the section index, ROI reversed out; the result as the headline, computed from the data; spot inks a hair out of register, a gold halftone under the leader's line, an agate table), 5 manga (an inked page: narration-box tabs, a cast strip with #1 on gold with focus lines and the loser in gloom tone, coloured lines over black ink, screentone below the start for money lost, the leader's gain as a sound effect). **Round 21 mockups, the proof (owner, 2026-10-23: five more, all very different, "if I can predict how it's going to look, you failed"; think about what makes people look, what matters most to them, and how a viewer becomes a follower on X or a buyer):** `ez/x2p-launch-r21-proof-{1,2}.png`, `ez/tools/concepts/launch-r21-proof.js` (numbers from ez-data.js and the page's ROI). Each answers the section's own question from a different angle, in a different printed object, and ends on an open loop printed on it (results still to come, the next 38 calls) that leads to X: 1 the coin test (a scratch card: each AI's hit rate and how often a fair coin does at least as well, one-sided binomial; mock shows two scratched, one half done, one untouched), 2 report card (first half vs second half of each AI's own calls in red pen, remarks worked out from the numbers, gold star for the most improved), 3 the cheque (a cheque to "you, if you copied" the ROI leader, then the ROI lines on greenbar paper with the leader in gold ink and the loser stamped RETURNED), 4 case file (an evidence board: the coin test, then vs now, the money, red string to "Too early to close. Leaning skill."), 5 scorecard (a baseball scorebook: last 10 rounds as innings, a right call a run, a wrong one a K, hit rate as a batting average). Measured on 42 graded rounds: Grok 22 of 34 (a coin does that 1 time in 16; 53% first half, 76% second), Claude 26 of 42 (1 in 12; 57% to 67%), GPT 23 of 42 (1 in 3; 52% to 57%; the ROI leader), Kimi 19 of 42 (below a coin; 52% to 38%). So the ROI leader (GPT) is the AI whose record fits luck best, while the AI we follow (Grok) has the strongest case for skill: gold means Grok in 1, 2, 4 and 5 and GPT in 3 (asked of the owner). Site class names that leak into mockups: `.pc` (standings rows), `.rs`, `.xg`, `.mk`, `.u`, `.d`, `.s`; prefix mockup classes. **Round 22, the ticket picked (owner, 2026-10-23: of the proof mockups "my favorite one... is the ticket", round 20 option 1, "but then it has to be for ROI, hit rate, average miss, and sector"):** `ez/x2p-launch-r22-ticket-views.png`, `ez/tools/concepts/launch-r22-ticket.js`: the bet ticket's card for all four views, the tab row in the order ROI, Hit rate, Avg miss, Sector (ROI first because it is the default), the open view's name foil-stamped; one rule for gold, the view's own #1 foil-stamped (ROI GPT, Hit rate and Avg miss Grok); each view its own band words (ROI "$100,000 on every call" / "Simulated · before fees"; Hit rate "Right on direction" / "Up or down only", the running hit rate per round on a labelled 25 to 100% scale with the coin flip dashed at 50%; Avg miss "How close they got" / "Closer is better", four targets printed in red ink, rings every 3% off, one ink dot per call, a red dot outside for a wrong direction, "% off" never "pts"; Sector "Where they are sharpest" / "Small samples · it fills in", the page's own grid printed with green, amber and red spot-ink tints). Mockups only, phone width; not on X2+ yet. **Round 23 (owner, 2026-10-23, on round 22; the proof opens on ROI, since the standings already show direction):** `ez/x2p-launch-r23-ticket-views.png`, `ez/tools/concepts/launch-r23-ticket.js` (round 22 kept as it was): gold only for the leader of the view on show (owner: "the gold needs to be specific to the one that is leading whichever section is being shown"), so the open tab is a solid ink box with the card's colour in its letters; all four tickets one height (the tallest view's inside, 452px at 390px; the chart or the sector grid takes the room, the note sits at the foot); on ROI and Hit rate the ranks 1 to 4 printed in each line's colour, the leader's in foil and its line the thickest (3.8px against 2px; since 2026-10-23, after the owner saw it on a phone, as thin as the rest: "because it's golden, it already stands out", the thick line hid what the others did; still gold, 2px with its dark wall and pale lip pulled in to half a pixel; live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-23, owner go); the line ends carry the logo and the model (MODELS[].ver: GPT-6 Astra, Kimi K3, Fable 5.1, Grok 4.7), not the name again; Hit rate starts every line at 50/50 at its first call ((right + 1) / (calls + 2), so the first call moves it to 67% or 33%; the line ends within a point of the raw rate in the ranking: Grok 63.9 vs 65%), on 30 to 80%, x from "Start" to the last round; Avg miss is target practice: rings every 3% off printed alternately in white ink and red ink with the card's grain, the % off in each ring, a fine crosshair, each call a bullet hole (dark, pale torn rim), a call that missed the direction a red dot outside. **On X2+ since 2026-10-23 (owner: "these look good. You can push them"; production waits):** x2plus.js `x2pProof` builds the ticket in the proof section (03) in place of the page's switch, which stays in the DOM, hidden (`#learn.x2p-ptk`); CSS in the PROOF block at the end of skin-x2plus.css (`__PAPER__` filled by build.py). It computes its numbers like the page: ROUNDS in order, the same $100,000 ROI sum (STAKE is not global on this page, so it keeps its own 100000), X2's ranking rule, the page's sector bands (2 in 3 right or better green, 1 in 3 or worse red) and short sector names (routes.js SECT_SHORT is not global either; x2pProof carries a copy). Opens on ROI; the four views share the tallest view's height at every width (503px at 360, 516px at 390 and 1280; measured), recomputed on fonts, load, width changes and tab changes; the Hit rate labels have no connector lines (owner: "too much noise"); ROI keeps them. 600px wide at most. Live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-23 (owner: "Everything"; until then make_site.py kept production on the page's own switch with `window.__x2pClassicProof`). A failure inside x2pProof is kept in `window.__x2pPtErr`. **Round 24 mockups, "cinematic cleanliness" (owner, 2026-10-23, with a reference film: a stylised 3D music video, "the art style is completely different... but the cleanliness is insane"):** `ez/x2p-launch-r24-cine.png`, `ez/tools/concepts/launch-r24-cine.js`, on the proof ticket now on X2+ (ROI), each take one principle read from the reference (one subject per shot, light doing the work, shallow focus, scale contrast, nothing extra): 1 key light (a warm light on the leader's line, the gold glowing into the paper, the card falling into shadow at its edges, the others quieter), 2 focus pull (the other lines out of focus), 3 close-up (the leader's money as the frame, the others one line; the ticket grows), 4 subtraction (crisp lines without the rough ink edge, the paper grain calmed, no dotted rules, stars, connectors or ticket number). Owner, same day: "on X2+ the current one is correct" (none of the four taken). **Highlights at the record's card size (owner, 2026-10-23: the record's cards "look at the same shape as a Pokemon card"; the highlights were "a bit bigger"; on the X2+ and X3+ artifacts and both preview branches; live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-23, owner: "Everything"):** the highlights carousel uses the record's sizing (58vw cards, 10px gap on phones; 330px, 18px gap, a 600px track from 760px), and eqH sets every highlight card to the record card's height, read from its box at full precision (a whole-pixel read left them up to 0.5px apart, a 3-decimal one 1/64px): measured equal to 0.0000px in width and height at 21 widths from 320 to 1920px, both sides, after a resize too (owner: "exact same specs in terms of height and width"). The story side is compact on phones (Stock moved and Winner side by side; the four call chips share one size, stepped down until the widest fits, 9.5px on a 360px phone; the paragraph steps down if it would overflow) and larger from 760px so it fills the taller card; nothing clips from 340px up (measured on all three cards; at 320px one chip runs 2px over at the 8.5px floor). The chip arrow sits on its number ("▼2.5%"). **Ticket board, round 25 (owner, 2026-10-23; X2+, X3+, both previews and, since the owner's "Everything" the same day, earningszn.xyz):** the Last 5 "more in the middle... room to breathe" and its boxes as far from the left line as from the right: the rows had a 6px column gap (12px from 760px) while each printed line is the next field's left border, so every value sat half a gap left of its field's middle (boxes 8px from one line, 13px from the other at 390px). Now no column gap, the hit rate field padded to the frame's inner line, and the header labels carry a clear 1px line like the fields: labels on their values to 0.01px, boxes equally far from both lines. The AI field is as wide as the longest name or model plus 16px (x2plus.js tixCols sets --x2p-aiw, rerun on fonts, load, resize and tab change), the last five take the rest with the boxes in its middle (the old board's "halfway between the names and the next column"); where there is no room (390px and narrower) the Last 5 keeps its 68px. ROI in the ticks' green ink when up (.pu), red ink when down (.pd), the dollar line in ink; #1's hit rate hot-foil stamped in GROK's gold ("red in investing means bad"), the others black. Known: at 320px the names still run into the Last 5 field (less than before). Owner, same day, on this board at a 390px phone: "this one is the one" (the Last 5 stays where it is; it does not move to the red line in the owner's earlier sketch). Intel (owner, same day: row 80 was wrong, it "reports next week"; later: "We need to change that"): Intel's own release says Thursday 29 October after the close; the sheet still says 10/22 and 10/23, so sync_sheet.py KEEPs 10/29 (call) and 10/30 (result) for R80 and prints a KEEP line on every run until the sheet carries them; live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-23 (production built from the live code, 3d40553, with the new data only). AmEx (R45) is in the sheet as called 10/23, result 10/24 (a Saturday); AmEx reported Friday 23 October before the open, so its result is that day's close (its calls were in the 10:33 UTC sync, before the 7:00 ET release); owner, same day: "It needs to resolve today", so the sync KEEPs 10/23 as its result date, live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-23. **Rules one line each (owner, 2026-10-23: "make this fit on one line, Graded at the next market close after earnings."; live on earningszn.xyz):** x2plus.js fitRules() steps the strap's three lines down together by half a pixel until each draws one line (it counts the lines each draws; the grid column grows with no-wrap text, so a line's own width cannot tell): 13px at 360px, 14px at 375px, 15px at 390 to 412px, the full 16.5px from 430px; on a 320px phone even 12px wraps, so it keeps the full size wrapped as before. Only the standings strap; the others keep 16.5px. **On X2+ the rules are back on that strap above the tickets (owner, 2026-10-23: "in the same format as we have it on the current live site"); the "Terms" small print under the board is gone.** **Names printed into the tickets (owner, 2026-10-23: the "As it stands" ticket and the bet ticket's "Bet #1: NVIDIA" still looked slapped on; "the same has to happen across the site", these two first; live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-23, owner go, while the ticket board stays on the preview):** the PRINTED block at the end of skin-x2plus.css, scoped to `html.x2p-prt` (x2plus.js sets it on every page): NVIDIA and the leader's name hot-foil stamped into the card (a satin gold gradient multiplied with the card's own grain, `__PAPER__`; sunk into the paper by a dark wall above each stroke and a pale lip below; the red drop, keyline and mirror rim are gone), "Bet #1:" and the ticket's words in ink that lets the card through; the "As it stands" ticket also gets printed rules above and below its line, its green and red bands printed in ink on the card instead of metallic foil, and the standings tickets' hard shadow (3px 4px, no blur). Mockups waiting for the owner: a smaller "Tap to play" and the bet ticket as wide as the film and a touch taller (`ez/x2p-launch-r6-first.png`); "last 5 rounds" under each row's boxes or "Last 5" in the header (`-last5.png`); the "As it stands" ticket on the bet ticket's paper at the board's full width, same words, Grok in gold (`-ticket.png`; owner: "the writing stays the same. Just the ticket and the font"). **"As it stands" ticket, picked (owner, 2026-10-22, after six rounds of mockups: full width was too loud, the bet ticket's twin too thick, carte blanche materials off the brand; pick 1 of the film-layout round, then "a bit more thick", Grok in one solid gold, the linen card's foil on the bands):** on the Overview it sits under the board, centred, 84% wide (max 440px; 94% below 390px), tilted -1.8deg, 44px tall, one line: the film ticket's layout (green band and stripe left, red right, scalloped sides) on lit cream card (paper relief from noise lit by a distant light, feTurbulence into feDiffuseLighting, as a data-URI background; every # in it escaped as %23 or the browser drops it), the words pressed in (letterpress text-shadow plus a slight ink displacement, #x2pInk), the bands in foil (metallic green and red gradients), Grok in NVIDIA's gold since the same day (owner: "the GROK looks a bit off", then picked the bet ticket's name style, "use this one"): x2plus.js copies the bet ticket's own rules for its name (gold mirror, cream lip, red shade, brown edge; from #x2pbt-css) onto `.x2p-ast .tk em`, so the two always match; the words in the bet ticket's ink (Anton capitals, 12.5px; 11.5px below 390px, 10.5px below 340px), GROK 19px, one line. The words are the page's own ("As it stands, we follow <leader> on our next bet."). x2plus.js builds `.x2p-ast` in the launch block; CSS at the end of skin-x2plus.css. Live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-22 (owner go). Rounds: `ez/x2p-launch-r6-ticket-*.png`. Mockups: `ez/x2p-launch-*.png`, `ez/tools/concepts/launch-two-tabs.js`, `launch-r6*.js`.
- **Proof rows and Upcoming, round 25 (owner, 2026-10-23):** on X2+, X3+, both previews and earningszn.xyz (live since 2026-10-23, owner: "push all that to production"): the proof's ROI and Hit rate rows print each name in its line's colour (Claude orange, Grok black, Kimi blue, GPT green; the view's leader stays gold foil), the ranks plain (1 in gold, 2 to 4 in ink), and the ROI money in green ink in profit, red at a loss, like the percentage beside it. Mockups waiting for the owner: `ez/x2p-launch-r25-proof.png`, `ez/tools/concepts/launch-r25-proof.js` (the money row with "the amount made" and room, and tabs that look tappable): A profit first (the gain, "+$27,511", is the big figure; value and % a small line; folder tabs standing off the ticket), B three columns (Now / Made / Return labelled once; raised buttons in a groove with "4 views · tap to switch"), C then and now (a bar per AI from the $100,000 line, right for profit, left for loss; a stepper "‹ ROI 1/4 ›" and "Next: Hit rate →"). Research behind them: money framing raised perceived returns (Diacon & Hasseldine 2007; a replication), dollar formats read riskier than the same figure in % (Nelson & Rupar 2015), brokerages mostly show both, so which one leads is unproven and a split test (the analytics' a/b bucket) can settle it; weak clickability cues cost users time (NN/g eyetracking). And `ez/x2p-launch-r25-upcoming.png`, `ez/tools/concepts/launch-r25-upcoming.js`, Upcoming in the ticket world ("the tickets become cards when they're graded"): 1 box office (each round an Admit One ticket like the standings tickets, Mag 7 stubs in gold foil, the calls stamped in once they are in, a three-step strip ticket → calls in → graded card), 2 take a number ("Now serving 43 · 44 · 45", then each day a roll of numbered tickets you swipe), 3 spotlight (today's rounds big under one warm light with their calls and the time to the close; the rest of the week one quiet line of logos per day), 4 pocket schedule (a folded printed schedule card, today highlighted, Mag 7 in red, ✓ where the calls are in). Big text is the ticker, or the company where the ticker is a number (000660.KS). The sheet writes some dates as 11/3/26 and 11/03/26; the page groups them as one day (checked).
- **Proof tabs as folders, round 26 (owner, 2026-10-23: of round 25 "from a selector point, it's the file one... it looks like a file, not like a button", and option B's rows "from an information perspective"; on X2+, X3+, both previews and, since the owner's "push all that to production" the same day, earningszn.xyz):** the four views are folder tabs of card stock standing off the ticket (the open one is the page itself: no fill, the card's stock under it hides the rule). Switching is "like switching folders... very smooth" (the owner's cinematic reference): the tapped tab rises 29 to 35px and the last one sinks back (260ms, cubic-bezier(.2,0,0,1)); the open page fades and sinks 4px in 120ms, the new one comes forward from 10px below and 98.5% scale in 280ms (Material's shared axis: a quick exit, about 300ms in, standard easing; 400ms is the ceiling); on ROI and Hit rate the lines then draw from their start (700ms; every series path has pathLength 1, `.pt-dr`), the end labels and connectors fade in after them, the rows and targets step in 40ms apart; the first time the ticket comes into view its lines draw once (IntersectionObserver); none of it with reduced motion (x2plus.js swap()/animate(), CSS beside the PROOF rows). A recording at phone width: `ez/x2p-proof-folders.mp4` (frames from the browser's own screencast at their real timing). ROI rows in three columns labelled once, Now, Profit, ROI: ROI (the percentage) the biggest (17px), Now and Profit 13px (the owner asked for the dollar column to be called ROI; the percentage is the ROI, so the percentage carries the name and the dollars are "Profit"); a narrower step below 380px and 340px so nothing wraps or clips from 320px up. **Standings, same round:** the spare width splits evenly between the Last 5 and the hit rate (both `minmax(...,1fr)`), so the Last 5 is narrower, ROI moves left at its own width and the hit rate gets room (owner's sketch at about 480px: at 500px both 120px, boxes and the hit rate centred in their fields); the boxes a size down (8x9px, the field from 68 to 58px at its narrowest), which at 390px gives the names their full 16px of air. **The top band, same day (owner: "too small", for every tab):** 31px tall, its words bigger. **Then the question each tab answers (owner, same day, jobs to be done: "when they read the thing in green", they know what the tab answers; live on earningszn.xyz, both artifacts and both previews since 2026-10-23, owner: "feel free to push to production"):** ROI "Would following them have made you money?", Hit rate "How often were they right about the direction?", Avg miss "How close did they get to the real move?" (both the owner's words), Sector "Which industries do they call best?"; one line, centred, sentence case (Barlow Condensed 700 14.5px; x2plus.js steps it down to fit, floor 11px), between two gold-foil stars (owner, same day: keep the stars, they mark it as the question being answered); no "Season 1". **Printed, not pasted on (owner, same day: the question, "Simulated · before fees" and the $100,000 line "feel like pasted on top"; live on earningszn.xyz, both artifacts and both previews since 2026-10-23):** the words in both bands are knocked out of the band's ink, so the bare card shows through (`.pt-ko`: the card's stock and grain clipped to the letters, a dark ink edge, a slight press displacement `#xptInk`); the bottom band 9.5px. The note under each view is the ticket's conditions: centred capitals under a printed double rule, in the ticket's ink, short sentences with full stops (a "·" list put a dot at the start of a line). The notes no longer repeat the questions. "$100,000 on every call" was wrong: the ROI is $100,000 at the start, then the whole balance on every call (compounding; buy on up, short on down, direction only, no leverage, before fees; a skipped round leaves the balance as it is), as X2's own "Each AI started with $100,000 on its first call and bet it all on every call since"; the ROI note now says so ("$100,000 to start. The whole balance on every call. Wins and losses carry over. Buy on up, short on down. No leverage."). **Line ends without logos (owner, 2026-10-23: "remove the logos... and the tiny little thing next to it"; the rows above carry the logos; live on earningszn.xyz, both artifacts and both previews the same day):** on ROI and Hit rate each line ends in its model's name in its colour at the line's height (moved apart only where two ends would touch), no logo and no connector line. **Mockups waiting (owner, same day: Now, Profit and ROI "always the same numbers"; proposed Start, ROI, Profit; "mock-ups first"):** `ez/x2p-proof-columns.png`, `ez/tools/concepts/launch-r27-columns.js` (the real page with its rows rewritten): A the owner's Start · ROI · Profit (Start is $100,000 in every row; clips at 320px as drawn), B ROI · Profit with "Each started with $100,000" once in the header, C Profit only, D ROI only. With a $100,000 start, profit and ROI always share their digits (+$44,000 = 44.0%), so any two of Now, Profit and ROI repeat; recommended D (one number per row, matches the tab's name), B if the dollar amount must show. **Round 28 (owner, same day: profit the largest number, ROI the same size as Start, Start as "$100K", profit in full; "start, ROI and then now, maybe give me some mockups"):** `ez/x2p-proof-columns-2.png`, `ez/tools/concepts/launch-r28-columns.js`: 1 Start · ROI · Profit (profit 17px, Start and ROI 13px), 2 Start · ROI · Now (Now 17px); both fit from 360px, at 320px the gold CLAUDE runs 1-2px past its column (needs a smaller name step there). Recommended 1: the profit is the direct answer to the band's question. **Round 29 (owner, same day: a tiny + and − in place of the triangles, "the 44% needs to be aligned on the ROI", Now may be a bit smaller as long as Start and ROI stay smaller; live on earningszn.xyz, both artifacts and both previews since 2026-10-23, owner: "looks good. push it to prod"):** `ez/x2p-proof-columns-3.png`: Start · ROI · Now, Start "$100K" in ink and ROI with a small fixed-width sign (`.pt-sg`) at one small size (13px; 11.5 below 380px, 10.5 below 340px), Now the biggest (17/15/13.5px) in green or red, each label centred over its numbers (measured within about 0.6px at 320, 360, 390 and 1280px; no overlap or clipping from 320px); x2plus.js V.roi, the ROI rows block in skin-x2plus.css.
- **Upcoming as fight night (owner, 2026-10-23, of the round 25 mockups: "my favorite one was graded at the close. And again, that's sort of one part of the narrative I want to keep across the page"):** on the X2+ and X3+ artifacts and both preview branches; **live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-23 (owner: "push pls"), with round 2 and round 3 below** (make_site.py no longer holds anything; `SITE_HOLD=1` sets `window.__x2pHold` for future held changes; see SYNC.md). x2plus.js `upTab` (CSS in the UPCOMING block at the end of skin-x2plus.css) replaces X2's timeline (kept in the DOM, hidden, so X2's routing still works): a spotlight under one warm light, "Tonight · Fri Oct 23" / "Graded at the close" / "In 2h 51m · 3 rounds · calls locked" (the time to 4pm New York, once a minute; after it "The bell has rung · results soon"; another day's close says its weekday instead of Tonight), the rounds with calls in as big Admit One tickets (the standings tickets' card stock, scallops, perforated stub "No. 43", the four calls stamped in, ▲ green and ▼ red ink); with no calls in, the next day's rounds as "Next on the card" with "calls due". Mag 7 stubs in gold foil with "Mag 7" under the number. A ticket opens the round's card as X2's tiles did. The slab label of a round with calls in reads **"Final grade on <date>"** (owner: "Graded on" read as already graded), on the record, the highlights and the pop-up. **No share buttons anywhere** (owner: "the sharing has to be a lot cleaner"; the NOSHARE block hides every `.shr`, the flip button takes the footer's width); the idea for later: share the graded card, or the card saying when it will be graded. **The standings' ⓘ** sits at the top right of the head's last letter (owner: "top right of the S", smaller): 16px, 4px after the S, its top on the capitals' top (x2plus.js capTop measures the baseline and the S's ascent; 0.00px off at 320, 390 and 1280), tap area 36px. **"How we rank" is a ticket** (owner: the back had "this old-school design"; the HOWTICKET block, `.x2p-tkb`): the proof ticket's card, scallops and hard shadow, "★ How we rank ★" in the green band in white ink, the same words printed in ink in a double frame with the numbers in red ink in printed boxes, "#1 makes the call" in gold foil, the ROI line as fine print, a red band "Season 1 · Nº <graded rounds>"; the board's size as before, no overflow from 320px up.
- **Visitor analytics (owner, 2026-10-22: "we need to start adding analytics"):** Umami Cloud (free Hobby plan, checked 2026-10-22: 100K events a month, 1 website, 6 months, custom events, funnels, no cookies so no consent banner). x2plus.js, last block: off until `UMAMI_ID` holds the Website ID (a public ID) and only on earningszn.xyz (data-domains too); page views sent by hand on load and on every tab change (the tabs live in the #); events once per page load: `section` (film, bet, record, standings, stats, highlights, get, when 40% is on screen), `film` (play, 25, 50, 75, end, corner, mute/unmute), `record` (swipe, tap), `highlights` (tap), `follow` (where: header/record/other). Every visitor gets a lasting bucket a/b (localStorage `ez-ab`, sent as the tag) for split tests. localStorage `ez-track-debug`=1 logs events to the console instead of sending. About 10 to 15 events a visit, so the free plan covers roughly 7,000 to 10,000 visits a month.
- **Only the finished page (owner, 2026-10-23: "whenever I reload the page, I see the old page... I want the current page to be the only page, not the makeover"; on both preview branches and both artifacts; live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-23, owner: "100% speeds it up... push it to the live site"; on the owner's phone the mark shows for about 2 to 3 s):** X2+ arrives covered (`html.x2p-cur`, set by build.py) with only the $EZ mark on the night ground (`#x2pLoad`, Anton's own outlines in `tools/v22/x2p-mark.svg`, so no font swap; it breathes, still with reduced motion); x2plus.js lifts the cover (a 0.2 s crossfade, `x2p-rv`) once the page is built (the new tabs, the film ticket and the bet ticket; on Upcoming or a shared round, the fight-night tab) and the first screen's fonts are in (asked for at once by build.py's script after `<body>`, `window.__x2pF`; at most 1.5 s more). Nets: 9.5 s after x2plus.js starts, 20 s from the start, and a `<noscript>` rule. Speed work in the same pass: fitTitles batched on X2+ (build.py; skips the hidden card title bars, refits only when the width changes), the record's name labels and the foil fitted in batches (x2plus.js nmFit, foilAll via the ResizeObserver), the shine and foil on cards off to the sides paused (SPEED block), the speed-line background on a fixed `body::before` layer. Measured on the real hosting at phone size: a fast phone sees the mark at about 0.8 s and the finished page at about 3 s (the live site shows the old layout at about 1.1 s and changes at 2.8 to 4.2 s); on a mid-range phone (4x slower processor) the longest freeze went from about 13 s to 3 s, while the finished page still takes about 10 s (building all 116 record cards is most of it; not changed). Every visible fitted size and foil position is identical to before (measured). Also fixed: a shared round (#/round/43) showed X2's old Upcoming list under its card and after it closed (place() treats a round address on Upcoming as Upcoming and builds it at DOMContentLoaded, not at load). The X3+ rip ticket says 1:20. The page says dark to the browser from the first byte (color-scheme dark, theme-color #0b0a14), so a phone's top bar no longer starts white.
- **Code review (owner, 2026-10-23/24: "please do a review of the code yourself right now"; everything live since the last review on 2026-10-22, read by risk, every number recomputed from the sheet, 33 page loads swept at 320, 390 and 1280px with the page's 12 silent catch blocks made to report; live on earningszn.xyz, both artifacts and both previews since 2026-10-24):** fixed, all three were mine from 2026-10-23: (1) the ROI rows' small sign had the Sector table's class (`pt-sg`, now `pt-pm`): the proof ticket's set-up threw (kept in `window.__x2pPtErr`), so it never refitted after fonts, rotation or resizing (stale height), and switching back to ROI threw again; (2) the SPEED rule named the record's cards as "#record .card", which build.py widens to the highlights and the pop-up, whose cards never carry `.cur`: every highlight held still (TSMC's rolling foil, the story side's holo); record-only rules now say `#car .card` (keep it that way); (3) idle work: the front card's unseen back, a turned card's unseen front and every Overview section off screen now hold their loops still (`.x2p-offs`, x2plus.js, with 200px of warning). Measured on a 4x slower phone: about 1.9 s of page work in every 2.5 s with nothing touched before, 0.7 to 0.9 s after where nothing loops on screen; what is on screen moves as before (checked loop by loop, flips included). Also: re-fits wait for a width change (a phone's address bar fires height-only resizes; they cost 60 to 90 ms of script and layout before, 10 to 25 ms after; my first "0.3 to 0.44 s per bar change" counted the loops' background work and was wrong). Checked and sound: the cover and its nets, page switching and old addresses, the round pop-up (Escape, close, focus), the Upcoming tab's New York close across the 1 November clock change (Intl), caching (page and data 60 s), every figure on the standings and the proof (recomputed from the raw sheet rows: hit rates 25/37, 29/45, 25/45, 21/45; balances $144,000, $137,314, $120,828, $66,983). Not changed, for later: the visible stats-side holo and the #1 foil animate background positions (style work every frame while in view; transforms would give the same look for less), the pop-up does not re-zoom when a phone turns, a rotation costs 2.6 to 3.2 s on a slow phone (X2's own re-fits), company logos sit under the year-long media cache (a corrected logo needs a new file name, as films do). The bet move shipped on 2026-10-24 (see "The bet at the foot"); a link like href="#bet" would be read as an address by the page's router (scroll with script instead).

Get $EZ is 06; build.py puts #x2pSt into X2's Overview order routine. The chip photo is lazy now, but it still loads on arrival (it sits on the first screen for a moment before the move); the Season 2 logos are lazy. The cover waits for the move (`x2pst`, or `window.__x2pBetErr`). Speed, found while shipping it: a CSS rule ending in a bare `*` after a class set on <html> (`.vb-ft .t *`) made the browser restyle the whole page, about 250 ms more before the cover lifted (name the elements instead; keep it that way everywhere); the section's text fitting forces full-page layouts, so it waits until the section is about a screen away (IntersectionObserver, 900px); and the skin already has `--x2p-paper` as a plain colour token on a stronger selector, so the paper texture is written in place (`__PAPER__`), never as that variable. Measured at 390px: the cover lifted at a median 2.73 s before and 3.47 s with the first port (6 runs each); after the fixes my own runs were noisy (other browsers shared the machine), and an independent review on a 4x slower processor (3 runs each, old build beside new) found the time to the cover lift and the load work the same as before; reaching the section costs about 25 ms of layout and 50 ms of script. The review's one defect, fixed before production: the frame's shadow layer was `<i class="sh">`, the site's section-head class, whose bottom margin cut the shadow 8 to 16px short (now `.vb-sh`). Not done: the chip photo's lazy loading saves nothing (see above); Safari not checked (filter with background-clip:text on the head and foot, as on the live proof bands). **Wording, same day (owner):** the proof strap's first line is "Do not trust the models. Verify the trend." (replaced "One lucky call proves nothing. The trend does."); the stakes' first two lines are "69% of $EZ creator fees fill the pool." and "The AI on top makes the call we trust." The owner dictated "69% of $EZ trading fees fill the pool for the bet"; flagged that pump.fun keeps most of each trade's fee (third-party guides citing pump.fun's 20 May 2026 schedule: bonding curve 1.25%, about 0.30% to the creator and 0.95% to pump.fun; after graduation the creator share runs 0.05% to 0.95% by market cap; not yet checked on pump.fun's own page), so 69% of what EZ receives is about 17% of all trading fees on the curve; the owner picked "creator fees". The explainer film still says "69% of the fees from every $EZ trade". **Every strap line one line (owner, same day: "it always needs to be one line"):** the stakes and the proof straps step down together like the rules strap (x2plus.js BETSEC fitLines, each fitted when it comes near): stakes 16.5px from 360px (14px at 320), proof 15px at 390, 14px at 360, 12px at 320. Keep new strap lines about as long as "Wrong call: the pool can be wiped out." (38 characters) so they keep their size. Live on earningszn.xyz, both artifacts and both previews since 2026-10-24. **Three lines at most (owner, 2026-10-25: "I want to have three lines max"; right and wrong "on the same line"; live on earningszn.xyz, both artifacts and both previews the same day):** the stakes are "69% of $EZ creator fees fill the pool." / "The pool gets bet on the top AI's call." (the owner's words; replaced "The AI on top makes the call we trust.") / "Right: $EZ buyback. Wrong: pool can be wiped out." ("Right:" green, "Wrong:" red, as the two separate lines had them; the longest line at 49 characters, so the strap's lines step down together: 16.5px from 430px, 14px at 390, 13px at 360, 11px at 320, where fitLines' floor for this strap is 11 instead of 12, so all three stay on one line).

On both artifacts and both previews; production waits. **Then (owner, same day, from a phone screenshot: "Next challenger" touched the field's right line; the challengers' names did not feel part of the ticket; "In tryouts" centred under it; "if next challenger just doesn't fit, you can just have next model"):** n5fit measures the text's own box (Safari let it run into the padding without growing scrollWidth) and keeps the ink 4px clear of the line, refitting whenever a web font finishes loading; below 12px the words become "Next model" (390 to 430px: "Next challenger" 13px, 13 to 18px clear; 360 and 375px: "Next model" 15px; 320: "Next model" 11px; 1280: "Next challenger" 17px); "In tryouts" centred under it (0.00px off, measured); the challengers' names as the tickets print a model ("Grok 4.7"): Barlow Condensed 600 11.5px (15 from 760px) in the grey ink, mixed case. On both artifacts and both previews; production waits. **Round 36 mockups (owner, same day: "only in tryouts and no next challenger", "models in tryouts", and "different colors, aesthetics of the tickets"; waiting for the owner):** `ez/x2p-launch-r36-tryouts.png` and `-desktop.png`, `ez/tools/concepts/launch-r36-tryouts.js` (the real page, Kimi's ticket above for comparison; the head is an `<em>`, since the page's n5fit rewrites any `<b>` in the field when a font loads): A "In tryouts" alone in the red head's place (17px, 25px clear of the line at 390), B "Models / in tryouts" on two lines, C "Models in tryouts" on one line (11px on a phone, too small); then "In tryouts" on five stocks: 1 kraft (brown ink), 2 chipboard (grey blank stock), 3 ink (black card, cream ink, gold rule, marks in cream), 4 training camp (field green, cream ink, gold head), 5 carbon copy (pink duplicate typed in carbon blue). No overflow, ticket heights equal to the others at 360, 390 and 1280px. **Picked and built (owner, same day: "let's take craft"; "you can have just like next models in tryouts... in the center again of the tiny little square"; on both artifacts and both previews, production waits):** the No. 5 ticket on kraft (option 1: `background-color:#c39a69` multiplied with the card's grain, dark brown ink, red label, a warm highlight under the words instead of the cream tickets' white one; the FIVE block), and in the AI field "Next models / in tryouts" on two lines in red Anton, centred in the printed square (the frame line sits 6px into the field, so 12px padding left and 6px right; measured 0.00 to 0.01px off centre at 320 to 1280px, both lines centred on each other), at the names' size (17px, 15 below 380px, 22 from 760px), n5fit stepping it down only if the longer line would come within 4px of a line (9px at 320). "Next challenger", "Next model" and the separate "In tryouts" line are gone. The owner asked whether "in tryouts" is understood beyond sports fans: said it is everyday English (school teams, auditions, "try out" a new phone), and "Next models" in front of it carries the meaning for anyone who has never heard it. **Then (owner, same day: "just models in tryouts... it sort of makes sense that they're next"; "make the in parentheses most valuable predictions the same size as MVPs"; "push the site as it is to production"):** "Models / in tryouts" (17px at 390, 22 on desktop, 11 at 320); the MVPs head's brackets at the head's own size (one line on desktop; on phones the brackets take the second line, every section head being 27px there; below 340px they may wrap). **Since 2026-10-25 (owner: "most valuable predictions needs to be next to MVPs... make it a tiny bit smaller so it fits"; live on earningszn.xyz, both artifacts and both previews):** one line at every width: x2plus.js steps the brackets down from 1em until the head fits its column (0.9em at 390px, 0.825em at 360, 0.675em at 320; full size from 430px), refitting on fonts, width changes and when the head comes near; only if .45em would not fit do they wrap at full size. **Live on earningszn.xyz, both artifacts and both previews since 2026-10-24**, with all of round 35 (pressed targets, stub flags, the kraft No. 5 ticket, spread the hand); live page checked at 390 and 1280px, no errors. **Round 37 mockups (owner, same day: the record's "See the stats" side "more like an actual card or an actual ticket using our colors from the branding kit", with a graded stamp that "look[s] like a real graded stamp"; the MVP back "a little tiny story written like a column", in a new order: the headline, the story right under it, then the calls, then Stock moved and Winner, the grade line at the bottom, with different art styles for its background; waiting for the owner):** `ez/x2p-launch-r37-record-backs.png` and `ez/x2p-launch-r37-mvp-backs.png`, `ez/tools/concepts/launch-r37-backs.js` (each option laid over the real back panel `.fin` of a real card, at 390px; data read from ROUNDS and the back's own rows; `_base.js` has `stamp()`, a round rubber stamp: double ring, "$EZ GRADING OFFICE" and "SEASON 1" round the rim, GRADED and the date in a box, worn ink, multiplied). Record (R45 AmEx): R1 ticket (standings stock, green band with the round knocked out, dates in printed boxes, printed rows, the close in a double frame, a red stamp struck across the dates, red foot band), R2 card back (grey-brown board in black and red ink, a stat table, a "Did you know?" line from the data, a green stamp), R3 grading slip (ruled form, typed, ticked boxes, red stamp on the grader's line), R4 ink card (black card, gold foil, an embossed gold seal). MVP (TSMC): M1 the column (a clipping from "The $EZ Tape", Pirata One masthead, Source Serif 4 headline and column with a red drop cap, an agate box score), M2 ticket, M3 the report (typed on a ruled index card, the grade as a red rectangular stamp), M4 fight night (the program's recap page, "The bell" and "Decision"). Nothing changed on the site. Mockup classes avoid the site's `.d`/`.u` (they painted red boxes behind the calls; renamed r37dn/r37up). Research not repeated: round 33 covered card backs and story backs, and these follow the brand's existing printed-paper language. **MVP backs picked: the recap (owner, 2026-10-25: "I like the recap", M4; on both artifacts and both previews; live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-25, owner: "Please push the production on the live site"; the record back is not picked yet):** x2plus.js hlSlabs writes every MVP story side as the fight program's recap page (`.x2p-hb.x2p-rc`; the RECAP block at the end of skin-x2plus.css): black card with the paper's grain (`__PAPER__` multiplied) and a gold inset rule, a red "Recap" bug with "Round <n> · <close date>", then the headline, the story right under it, the four calls (the called move in red or green ink by its direction, ✓ green or ✗ red after it), "The bell" (the stock's move) and "Decision" (the winner, "Nobody" when none) side by side over gold rules, flush left, the grade line last, "See the film" at the foot. The record's three holo levels on the story side still apply. Type a size up from the mockup to use the card's room (phone: head 18px, story 13.5px; desktop: 27px, 19px); eqH still steps the story and the chips down if they would overflow. Checked at 320, 360, 390 and 1280px on X2+ and X3+: no errors, nothing overflows from 360px up (at 320px the chips sit at their 8.5px floor as before). Mistake on the way: after a context reset two publishes went to new artifacts (https://claude.ai/artifact/RL9ckLGTF4ZGkwAJLvvUBQ and https://claude.ai/artifact/HqoBFpS876yvfKTEmnZcg6, private) before the real X2+ and X3+ links were republished; they are strays, left in place until the owner says to delete them. They have no data or media files (only the page), so they never get past the $EZ mark; on 2026-10-25 the owner reported X2+ "not loading", most likely one of them (same title, newest in the gallery), while the real link holds all 239 files. After a reset, always publish with the artifact's `url`. **Round 38 mockups, the MVP copy (owner, 2026-10-25: the story side reads like "some stats"; it must make people go "wow": how rare an MVP is, how badly a miss missed, the competition between the AIs, AIs failing and AIs looking superhuman, the odds, sarcasm, "a bit unhinged"; five formats with a newspaper or ticket feel; the old trading cards' "1 in 500" with a little star bottom right; waiting for the owner):** `ez/x2p-launch-r38-mvp-{1,2}.png`, `ez/tools/concepts/launch-r38-mvp.js` and `launch-r38-mvp/` (`_facts.js` computes every number from ROUNDS, as the real build would; each format is laid over the real back of the three MVP cards at 390px; nothing overflows): A tabloid ("The $EZ Tape", Extra), B betting slip (PAID / LOST stamped), C ringside recap (today's recap rewritten, tale of the tape), D wire telegram (pasted strips, STOP), E case file (kraft folder, typed fields, an analyst's note, a stamp); each carries ★ and the odds bottom right. The facts behind the copy (45 graded rounds, 172 calls): a typical call misses by 3.25% (median), a right call by 2.55% on average; bullseyes (right way, within 0.1%) 3 of 172 = 1 in 57; Grok's Tesla call 0.02% off, about 160 times closer than a typical call, while GPT and Kimi called Tesla up; Claude and Kimi have no bullseye yet; TSMC: all four right, nobody more than 1.71% off, average 0.91%, Claude beat Kimi by 0.02% (0.49 vs 0.51); Pristine 10s 2 of 45 rounds (TSMC, RPM), shown as 1 in 23; Accenture +16.02%, the biggest move of the season, 3.9 times the average move, and the season's four worst calls are all from that round (20.02 to 22.02% off, Claude worst); in the 37 rounds all four AIs called, all four were wrong together 3 times (1 in 12), against about 1 in 33 if each thought for itself (an overall wrong rate of 72 of 172), so they miss as a pack. Mockup class names carry a q prefix: the site's own `.ft`, `.fd` and `.st` broke the first render (`.ft` drew a dark pill). **Picked and built (owner, 2026-10-25: "the tabloid extra... in terms of the text... the ringside recap... from an aesthetic standpoint"; on both artifacts and both previews, production waits for the owner's go):** x2plus.js hlSlabs writes every MVP story side with the tabloid's words in the ringside look: the Recap bug and round, a gold Anton headline with its key words in red ("Grok calls it to the decimal", "Four AIs, one brain", "Accenture fools everyone"), an italic cream deck ("Said Tesla −3.1%. It fell 3.08%."), the story with the odds and the sarcasm, the tale of the tape over a gold rule (`.x2p-tt`: the bullseye's miss vs the typical miss; the round's average miss vs a normal right call; the move vs how many times a normal move), the calls as a light grid (still `.x2p-hc`, so the holo levels keep working), and a foot with "See the film" left and the rarity right (`.x2p-rar`, ★ "1 in N", its meaning in aria-label and title: bullseyes 1 in 57 calls, the round's grade word among all rounds (Pristine 1 in 23), the biggest move 1 in 45 rounds). The Decision and grade lines are gone from the back (the label above carries the grade; the owner had asked to keep the grade line in round 37, so flagged). Templates fall back to plain wording for cases the data does not have yet: a closest call that is not a bullseye ("gets closest"), a bullseye with no rival the other way ("Everyone got the direction. The next closest was 0.62% off."), a tie at the top, a biggest move some AIs got right ("breaks the scale"). All numbers come from sf() (computed once from ROUNDS; per-call misses from the sheet's own column, as ezGrade). eqH now checks the back face against the card's height (the page sizes a card to its content, so the old overflow test never fired for a longer story) and, when the story is still too tall at its 10px floor, steps the headline and deck down too: Accenture's story is 12px at 390px, 10px at 360 and 320; nothing overflows at 320, 360, 390 and 1280px on X2+ and X3+, no errors. CSS: the round 38 rules inside the RECAP block of skin-x2plus.css. **Context over numbers (owner, 2026-10-25, later: "how do you contextualize the numbers... why it is impressive"; highlight the most relevant elements, not only numbers; "think about it this way" with a metaphor or analogy most people relate to; on both artifacts and both previews, production waits):** the story is now one or two sentences with the key facts in bright bold ("GPT and Kimi said up. Grok said down, and nailed it."; "Everyone right. Everyone close. Claude edged Kimi for the win."; "The biggest move of the season, and the four worst calls of it, all in one day."), then a "Think of it this way" block (`.x2p-tkw`: a gold label, a gold rule on the left, italic, its key words in gold; never `.x2p-tk`, which is a site ticket class and drew scallops and paper behind it). The comparisons are worked out from the same numbers: a miss is a share of the stock's price, so it scales to guessing a stranger's height of 1.80 m (a typical call, 3.25%, is off by about 5.9 cm, "a thumb"; Grok's 0.02% by 0.36 mm, "four sheets of paper" at 0.1 mm a sheet; x2plus.js hgt() picks sheets of paper, a pencil's width, a coin, a thumb, a hand's width or a ruler by size); TSMC's winning margin of 0.02% scales to a 100 m photo finish won by 2 cm; Accenture: "Four weather apps forecast drizzle. A hurricane shows up." and, only while all four AIs miss together more often than chance says (3 of 37 against about 1 in 33), "like four students who 'studied alone' and hand in the same wrong answer". The tale-of-the-tape number box is gone (the owner: "instead of showing numbers"; the deck under the headline keeps the call and the result). Fits: 12.5px on a 390px phone (Accenture 12px; 11px at 360), 17px on desktop, nothing overflows from 320px, no errors on X2+ and X3+. Board: `ez/x2p-launch-r39-analogies.png`. Owner, same day: "yes, this is the right direction", then "can you push that live as well?": live on earningszn.xyz since 2026-10-25. **Round 40 mockups, the record cards' stats side (owner, 2026-10-25, on R43 Bloom Energy: the label's "2 of 4 right · 7.19% off on avg" should be noticed more; the back should feel like a "stamp of approval", rooted in research; answers to my questions: the result big, three options on today's content and five carte blanche, directions my call, the grade on the label only; waiting for the owner):** `ez/x2p-launch-r40-recback-{1,2}.png`, `ez/tools/concepts/launch-r40-recback.js` and `launch-r40-recback/` (laid over the real back of round 43 at 390px; data from ROUNDS; nothing overflows). On every option the label's line becomes "2 OF 4 RIGHT" in Anton 17px with "· 7.19% off on avg" small after it (fits at 360 and 390). Same content: S1 grading report (cream paper, a black "$EZ Grading Office · Report No. 0043" band, the dates in printed fields, a blue APPROVED seal with a milled rim and the office round the rim, a verify line), S2 seal in the paper (today's dark back with the office seal blind-embossed large behind the rows and a mint APPROVED stamp where GRADED sits), S3 certificate stock (bank-note green, a guilloche rosette, a gold double frame, the winner in gold foil, a gold seal). Carte blanche: C1 subgrades (the market's close as a gold strip on top, each AI a Beckett-style subgrade box with its call and why: Winner and how far off, its rank and how far off, or Wrong way), C2 inspected (white butcher paper, one purple ink, a grader's shield per call, PASSED or REJECT; the USDA purple ink is from memory, not checked), C3 passport page (ADMITTED in a green rectangle or DENIED in a red oval per AI, the close as "border control"), C4 exam paper (answers in blue ink, marked in red pen, the answer circled, a MARKED stamp), C5 certificate of authenticity (cert number and verify line, the calls as a register, a signature and a holographic $EZ sticker; the signature "A. Grader" is invented). Research (two helpers): Beckett codes its label colour by grade (black for a Pristine 10), CGC by label type, every certificate carries a cert number tied to a lookup; seals read as official through hard borders, milled rims and the office round the rim; NN/g: at most three type sizes, the most important the largest, one feature that pops out is seen pre-attentively; WebKit has no GPU path for SVG filters, so a build should draw the stamps and textures once (shared images) rather than as live filters on 120 cards. Class names carry an option prefix (s1-, c2- ...): the site's own `.ft`, `.fd`, `.st`, `.x2p-tk` broke earlier mockups. (3) **Bullseye stack motion** (owner: "spread the hand is probably the best one... or peek and tuck... the least heavy on site performance"): `ez/x2p-launch-r34-stack-{1..4}.mp4`, `launch-r34-stack/o1..o4`. Measured at 390px on a 4x slower CPU, three runs each, medians (`launch-r34-stack` engine, trace of the renderer's main thread): the cue as the stack comes to the middle costs the page's own 2.54 s of work in that window plus 0.65 s for peek and tuck (12 fps against 27) and 0.22 s for spread the hand (19 fps); the taps add nothing outside run-to-run noise for either; neither keeps working afterwards. So spread the hand is both the pick and the lighter one. Its mockup had a bug, fixed 2026-10-24 and re-recorded: the page makes the cards behind the top one `inert`, so in the open hand only the top card took taps; a spread hand must lift `inert` and `aria-hidden` on all three and put them back on close (x2plus.js lay() does it after a pick). When building it: brightness is animated as a filter in the mockups; an overlay's opacity would keep it on the compositor in every browser (Safari not measured).
- **Round 41 mockups, five takes on S1 (owner, 2026-10-25: S1 grading report is the favourite; "2 of 4 right" a bit too big; keep today's crown; the office band in the site gold, or one with only "Report No. 0043" like a filing cabinet; keep the verify link (to that card, #/round/n); different stamps, papers and stamp places; one with the winner all in gold, one with just a crown; PASS/REJECT or PASS/FAIL from C2 so it is clear how many got the direction, in brand colours, not a shield; everything integrated; an homage to old grading and approval marks for a world where you track the AI rather than trust it; waiting for the owner):** `ez/x2p-launch-r41-s1-iterations.png` (390px, with today's back and S1), `-desktop.png`; files `ez/tools/concepts/launch-r40-recback/s1a..s1e` through `launch-r40-recback.js` (shared R41 helpers at the end of `_rows.js`: a gold-foil crown, small worn ink stamps rect/oval/cart/solid; the label line is 13px now in `_base.css`). A gold office (gold band, crown + Winner tag printed, PASS/FAIL boxes, "2 pass · 2 fail", green seal at the foot, rim "TRACKED · NOT TRUSTED"), B filing cabinet (manila folder, tab "Report No. 0043", no office name, a GRADED date stamp at the top in place of the graded field, crown only, PASS/REJECT ovals), C winner in gold (white stock, office name in foil, the whole winning row hot-foil stamped, solid PASS/FAIL blocks, gold certificate seal top right; "Winner" dropped below 380px), D hallmark (assay hallmarks struck into grey board: $EZ, 2/4, crown, R43, "Tested by the market, not the maker."; PASS/FAIL in clipped-corner cartouches; crown only), E inspection sheet (green ledger, PASS/REJECT tick columns with totals, crown + Winner, red GRADED stamp bottom left, rim "DON'T TRUST · TRACK"). The stamp word is GRADED or VERIFIED, never PASSED/APPROVED on a split round (the stamp vouches for the grading, not the AIs). Research: approval and notary seals go by the signature at the foot and never over text (NNA; N.C. G.S. 10B-36); received/filed date stamps go at the top. S1's blue stamp is not a brand colour either (all five use green, red or gold). At 1280px the content keeps its phone size inside the taller card (the build must scale it); no overflow at 360, 390 or 1280px.
- **Round 42 mockups, the certificate (owner, 2026-10-25, on round 41: a combo; waiting for the owner):** the head is S3's "★ Certificate of Grading ★" (or "★ Certificate ★"; "Grading certificate" is the USDA's own term for its official grading document, which must carry the certificate number, the date of grading and the grader, as ours does: "No. 0043", the graded date, "Grader: the market close"), no "$EZ Grading Office" band; C's white stock; B's typed "Calls filed" box; C's gold seal (GRADED and the date); ONE winner mark only (owner: crown + gold line + Winner was too much): the crown, the gold line or the word; PASS/FAIL in D's hallmark cartouches, or E's PASS/REJECT tick columns with totals. `ez/x2p-launch-r42-certificate.png`: 1 crown + hallmarks (seal top, beside Calls filed), 2 gold line + hallmarks ("Certificate"), 3 the word Winner + hallmarks, both dates typed, seal at the foot ("Grading Certificate"), 4 crown + ticks, 5 gold line + ticks, seal at the foot. Files: `launch-r40-recback/ka..ke.js` call `R42.build(D,{head,win,mark,seal})` (end of `_rows.js`; CSS at the end of `_base.css`, classes `xcf-`). The site already uses `.cg`, `.cg-r` (the sector grid), `.top` (the header) and `.tick` (the ticker): the first try drew the logos tiny and black squares behind the seal and the ticks. Nothing overflows at 360 and 390px; desktop not shot (the content would keep its phone size inside the taller card, as in round 41).
